Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The nuclear_physics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 825 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 977 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nuclear_physics decomposition. By minimality, the element a^825 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 977-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
